Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Jun 25 2014 15:58:00 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

The most basic problem with FDI --- repatriation i.e. FDI is investment with CONDITIONS and it is NOT unconditional investment
.
Consider two situation (1) Say an investor from Mumbai comes and wants to invest in Ahmedabad, then should people in Ahmedabad oppose? (2) Say an investor from USA comes and wants to invest in India, Then should people in India oppose?
.
Or the question can come as ---- Why should anyone oppose foreign investor? After all, he is an investor., and he is the one who will be facing the risks
.
Let me re-state first question. Say an investor from Mumbai comes and wants to invest in Ahmedabad, then should people in Ahmedabad oppose? NO. People in Ahmedabad can only gain and have NOTHING to lose. But lets say investor puts a conditions before people of Gujarat that "he will invest 1000 kg of gold , and people of ahmedabad will have to give him gold equivalent for profits he makes !!". The should people of Ahmedabad accept his conditions? Now there a catch -- what if people in Ahmedabad dont have gold to repay his profits? Then they will end up selling all their land, rivers etc etc.
.
FDI is not just investment --- but it comes WITH CONDITIONS ON nation in which investments are made. And the most damage causing condition is that GoI will have to get dollars to investor for the profits he makes. And GoI has to get dollars anyhow. And what if nation or Govt doesnt have dollars to repay at that time? Then nation will have practically no option but to sell away all its forests, mineral mines, rivers, ponds, plots, monopoly like railways, airports, roads etc etc. "Repatriation" i.e. commitment to give back dollars is a MUST condition in almost all FDI deals. Now there is no international law that FDI must have repatriation clauses. But almost all FDI deals have this written explicitly or implicitly.
.
Other damaging clauses FDI may have is tax exemption. eg FDI or FII from Mauritius is exempt from capital gains. Next FDI in railways is worst --- the Japense will ask and get monopoly over tracks they make , i.e. no Indian company will be allowed to put similar tracks for next several years !! Each FDI needs to be studied separately. But a common theme on all FDI is that it it binds nation and govt with condition of repatriation. FDI should be renamed as 'Investment with reparation condition".
.
Now apex leaders of BJP-Congress-RSS-AAP do NOT give this information to activists and voters, because they all heavily depend on mediamen who are all FDI funded. AAP is 100% a creation of FDI-paid-media. Congress may have had a good-bad past, but since 1991, Congress-apex has been MNC-puppet. And BJP is too becoming more and more dependent on FDI-paid-mediamen. And so their leaders ont talk about repatriation conditions inherent in FDI and damage it can cause.
.
So solution I propose is that we should inform fellow activists and citizens as much as possible about this repatriation condition which comes with FDI. More the number of people who know this condition, more difficult it will become to for Sonia-NaMo-AK etc to pass this poison pill of FDI to voters.
.
And finally long term solution is --- we need to improve our general manufacturing because thats the only way we can improve weapon manufacturing. Otherwise, we cant stop coming armies --- be Chinese or American. We can stop FDI by legislations, but we cant stop armies by legislations. And to stop invading armies, we need weapons --- again, weapons which are 100% made in India and made by India. Not some FDI. For that, we need to reduce inefficiencies of our factory owners. And for that we need law-drafts such as Jury System, wealth tax, TCP, Right to recall, etc etc . So please inform fellow citizens about these drafts, and also inform that Congress-BJP-AAP etc are anti-Jury, anti-wealth-tax,. pro-VAT, pro-GST, pro-Service-tax, anti-Inheritance-tax, anti-RTR etc. Informing voters and activists about Congress-BJP-AAP is necessary so that they dont end up wasting their time,
.

.

.
(c) And only Japanese trains will run on it, and if any other trains operate then they will have to pay toll to Japanese. company which owns these tracks.
.
(d) No Indian or other company will be allowed to lay such tracks and run such trains for next several years
.
(e) And GoI will be required to pay dollars for profits this monopoly makes !!!
.
(f) Plus Japanse may ask and manage to get tax exemption for several years.
.
This is all unofficial info. Official info is yet to come.
.

.

If Sonia\MMS had given away rights to lay down tracks to Japanese, the workers of RSS\BJP\BST etc would have shouted sell out and agitated in street and stopped the deals. But they are now all shamelessly supporting FDI in railways. Bunch of time-wasting activists.
;.
And please makde no mistake, AK is opposing all law-drafts needed to improve railways and this AK in effect is supporting FDI in railways,. AK has also said that AAP MPs will NOT submit any private member's bill in Parliament to stop FDI in railways.
.
Solution I propose is that we should ask voters to send orders to MPs via SMS to print Legislation in Gazette to stop FDI in railways., and also print RTR-Railway-Minister and Jury System over Railway Staff draft in Gazette.

Wed Jun 25 2014 17:11:16 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Why "honest" PM will NOT suffice and why we need good law-drafts? Because even if PM is "honest", MNC-owners can threaten him with war via Pakistan if he doesnt allow MNC-owners to takeover Indian minerals, Indian monopolies etc.
.
NaMo-andhbhagats and AK-andhbhagats since 2011 have been singing a cacophony that "all we need an honest tall leader, and there is no need for good law-drafts". i.e. "just install NaMo or AK as PM, and there is no need for RTR-law-draft, Jury-law-draft etc etc". And some year ago, I had explained that "honest" PM will NOT suffice. I will try to dig that facebook status out in few days. But I will repost the summary.
.
The issue is not just corruption, but raw threats given by MNC-owners of indirect invasion via Pakistan. Let me give example. In 1998, ABV said no to MNC-owners demand to allow US insurance companies into India. And ABV defied USA and went for Pokharan-2. So US Govt paid big bucks to pakistan, provided weapons and satellite information which enabled Pakistan to do a Kargil on India. And war stopped ONLY after India accepted all US conditions !!
.
So same way, if India's PM doesnt hand over cash making monopolies to like Mumbai-Ahmedabad corridor to MNC-owners, then Kargil-2 can happen with 10 times the force, Or wise, MNC-owners can block supply of weapons when China invades us. Now given that Americans dont have developed railway technologies, the cake passed to Japanese MNC-owners. Thats just a side fact, But core fact behind is that MNC-owners can use threat of blocking weapon supply and/or giving weapons to Pakistan , and then force honest PM to hand over economies.
.
So solution is to improve manufacturing in general, and improve weapon manufacturing in particular,. This can be done NOT by some honest tall leader, but by printing dozens of necessary law-drafts. The USA has better manufacturing than India, not because they had a NaMo or an AK or a dozen NAMo or a dozen AK. USA has better manufacturing ONLY because they have 10s and 100s of better law-drafts such as RTR, Jury, wealth tax, inheritance tax, have less sales tax, dont have VAT\Service-Tax\GST etc etc. And we need to have same law-drafts -- there are no short cuts.
.
Now NaMo-andhbhagats can see for themselves. They have honest PM, a line like PM and all that. And yet we are seeing 100% FDI in railways, FDi in college education, FDI in newspapersm FDI in defence and what not. As fr as FDI goes, NaMo is acting the same way MSS]Sonia did. The issue is NOT honesty - the point is strength. The nation has no Milutary strength left and so no PM can so no to MNC-owners who have all weapons of all kinds. And before AAPians start using this paragraph , let me tell this --- AK would have NO different from NaMo]Sonia on FDi matter. In facf, AK himself is FDI.
.
So wake and smell coffee. Please end your NaMo-bhakti , AK-bhakti etc, and please start campaiging for good law-drafts such as TCP, JurySys, RTR, wealth tax etc etc.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Wed Jun 25 2014 21:01:22 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

एक समय की बात है मकान्धी नामक देश पर मंदासुर नाम का राजा शासन करता था । मकान्धी प्राकृतिक सम्पदा जैसे जमीन, जल, वन, प्रकाश, खनिज आदि से संपन्न राज्य था । मंदासुर ने मकान्धी को और अधिक वैभव शाली बनाने के लिए तथा मकान्धी की प्रजा की भोग लिप्सा के शमन हेतु कुछ व्यापारियों को सूदूर देश से आमंत्रित किया । व्यापारियों ने मंदासुर को ट्रक भर के 1 करोड़ स्वर्ण मुद्राओं का भुगतान किया और बदले में मकान्धी की 60 करोड़ लोह मुद्राये प्राप्त की । मंदासुर ने व्यापारियों को वचन दिया कि वे मकान्धी क्षेत्र में व्यापार द्वारा जो भी लाभ लोह मुद्राओं में कमाएंगे उसके बदले स्वर्ण मुद्राये मंदासुर उन्हें दे देगा ।

व्यापरियो ने अपनी बुद्धिमता , तकनीक और चातुर्य से तरक्की की और खूब फले फूले । मंदासुर ने 1 करोड़ स्वर्ण मुद्राओं को मकान्धी के नागरिको की सुविधाओं पर खर्च किया जिस से मंदासुर की कीर्ति सभी दिशाओं में फैलने लगी ।
हर कोई खुश था ।

व्यापारी खुश थे , उन्होंने बहुत मुनाफा कमाया।
मंदासुर अपनी वाह वाही और मकान्धी का वैभव देख कर खुश था।
नागरिक वैभव पाकर खुश थे ।

बीस वर्ष पश्चात् व्यापारियों ने मंदासुर से कहा कि हमने जो लाभ अर्जित किया है वो हम अपने देश में लेकर जाना चाहते है अत: आप हमारे द्वारा अर्जित मकान्धी की मुद्रा लेकर हमे अपने देश की मुद्रा दे दीजिये ।

व्यापारियों ने 6000 करोड़ लोह मुद्राये 6000 ट्रको में भर कर मंदासुर को दी तथा बदले में 100 करोड़ स्वर्ण मुद्राये देने को कहा ।
मंदासुर के पास इतनी अधिक मात्रा में स्वर्ण मुद्राये नही थी न ही ऐसा कोई स्त्रोत था जिस से वो इतनी बड़ी संख्या में स्वर्ण हासिल कर सके ।

व्यापारियों ने स्वर्ण मुद्राये नही देने पर उनके देश के बादशाह से न्याय की गुहार की ।
बादशाह दानवसुर बहुत शक्तिशाली था इसलिए मंदासुर को मकान्धी को आक्रमण से बचाने के लिए दानवसुर के सामने समर्पण करना पड़ा ।

समझौते के अनुसार मकान्धी की उपजाऊ और कीमती जमीनों पर , खनिज क्षेत्रो पर व्यापारियों का एवं दानवसुर का तब तक अधिकार रहेगा जब तक कि उनकी 100 करोड़ स्वर्ण मुद्राओं की भरपाई नही हो जाती ।
तथा नागरिक विद्रोह को रोकने और शान्ति स्थापित करने के लिए दानवसुर ने अपनी सेना की तैनाती माकान्धी की भूमि पर कर दी ।

इस प्रकार मकान्धी भी जूपन, कुरियन, अरबियन, लिबियन, इर्कियन की तरह दानवसुर के उपनिवेश में परिवर्तित हो गया ।

*वे 6000 लोह मुद्राओ से भरे ट्रक अभी भी मंदासुर के राजभवन में खड़े है ।जिनका प्रयोग मंदासुर का मंत्रिमंडल सितौलिया खेलने में करता है ।

पात्र:
मंदासुर - मोदी
मकान्धी - भारत
व्यापारी - FDI
स्वर्णमुद्रा - डॉलर
लोहमुद्रा - रुपया
दानवसुर - बराक ओबामा
नागरिक - FDI के समर्थक

प्रजा अधीन राजा @ pk

एक समय की बात है मकान्धी नामक देश पर मंदासुर नाम का राजा शासन करता था । मकान्धी प्राकृतिक सम्पदा जैसे जमीन, जल, वन, प्रकाश, खनिज आदि से संपन्न राज्य था । मंदासुर ने मकान्धी को और अधिक वैभव शाली बनाने के लिए तथा मकान्धी की प्रजा की भोग लिप्सा के शमन हेतु कुछ व्यापारियों को सूदूर देश से आमंत्रित किया । व्यापारियों ने मंदासुर को ट्रक भर के 1 करोड़ स्वर्ण मुद्राओं का भुगतान किया और बदले में मकान्धी की 60 करोड़ लोह मुद्राये प्राप्त की । मंदासुर ने व्यापारियों को वचन दिया कि वे मकान्धी क्षेत्र में व्यापार द्वारा जो भी लाभ लोह मुद्राओं में कमाएंगे उसके बदले स्वर्ण मुद्राये मंदासुर उन्हें दे देगा ।

व्यापरियो ने अपनी बुद्धिमता , तकनीक और चातुर्य से तरक्की की और खूब फले फूले । मंदासुर ने 1 करोड़ स्वर्ण मुद्राओं को मकान्धी के नागरिको की सुविधाओं पर खर्च किया जिस से मंदासुर की कीर्ति सभी दिशाओं में फैलने लगी ।
हर कोई खुश था ।

व्यापारी खुश थे , उन्होंने बहुत मुनाफा कमाया।
मंदासुर अपनी वाह वाही और मकान्धी का वैभव देख कर खुश था।
नागरिक वैभव पाकर खुश थे ।

बीस वर्ष पश्चात् व्यापारियों ने मंदासुर से कहा कि हमने जो लाभ अर्जित किया है वो हम अपने देश में लेकर जाना चाहते है अत: आप हमारे द्वारा अर्जित मकान्धी की मुद्रा लेकर हमे अपने देश की मुद्रा दे दीजिये ।

व्यापारियों ने 6000 करोड़ लोह मुद्राये 6000 ट्रको में भर कर मंदासुर को दी तथा बदले में 100 करोड़ स्वर्ण मुद्राये देने को कहा ।
मंदासुर के पास इतनी अधिक मात्रा में स्वर्ण मुद्राये नही थी न ही ऐसा कोई स्त्रोत था जिस से वो इतनी बड़ी संख्या में स्वर्ण हासिल कर सके ।

व्यापारियों ने स्वर्ण मुद्राये नही देने पर उनके देश के बादशाह से न्याय की गुहार की ।
बादशाह दानवसुर बहुत शक्तिशाली था इसलिए मंदासुर को मकान्धी को आक्रमण से बचाने के लिए दानवसुर के सामने समर्पण करना पड़ा ।

समझौते के अनुसार मकान्धी की उपजाऊ और कीमती जमीनों पर , खनिज क्षेत्रो पर व्यापारियों का एवं दानवसुर का तब तक अधिकार रहेगा जब तक कि उनकी 100 करोड़ स्वर्ण मुद्राओं की भरपाई नही हो जाती ।
तथा नागरिक विद्रोह को रोकने और शान्ति स्थापित करने के लिए दानवसुर ने अपनी सेना की तैनाती माकान्धी की भूमि पर कर दी ।

Thu Jun 26 2014 02:49:24 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

1. NaMo gives approvals to Chinese Industrial Parks. i.e. Chinese will get several plots across India of sizes running 100s of acres , where ONLY Chinese can setup factories !!! Most Indian courts will have jurisdiction, but some Indian courts such as Indian labor courts, PF laws etc have no jurisdiction there. !!! More details will come on after during budget speeches. And AK, Sonia are supporting this move.
.
2. Old news from Ukraine --- Ukraine rents out 5% of its land to Chinese !! Can that happen in India?
.
3. Why is youth and average India of India pro-FDI? How Sonia-NaMo-AK and workers of Congress-RSS-BJP-AAP-BST etc have convinced average Indian and youth that only foreigners can build India?
.
========================
.
1. NaMo hands over 5 huge plots inside India to Chinese
.
Please see link in first comment. The Business Standard newspaper says that NaMo has given approval to Chinese to set up 5 industrial parks across India. One such park's approval has already been given all approvals in Gujarat. In these industrial parks, a huge chunk of land , running into 1000-more acres is given to Chinese. The details are yet to come. But here are some possibilities.. PLEASE NOTE THAT FOLLOWING ARE THE POSSIBILITIES
.
(a) whatever profits Chinese companies make here, GoI will have to give the dollars for profits in return !!
.
(b) If the decide to keep profits in India, then that profit will be tax exempt
.
(c) ONLY Chinese can setup factories there.
.
(d) If an Indian wishes to setup factory, restaurant or anything,, then that Indian will need permission of Chinese body which "owns" that plot.
.
(e) And inside that plot, all municipal services such as water, roads, internal buses, garbage, sewere etc will be run by Chinese companies.
.
(f) The Municipalities will have no jurisdiction and can collect property taxes there !!
.
(g). And they wont need to pay stamp duty to State Govt for internal sales !!
.
(h) While jurisdiction of most Indian courts will apply, many courts such as labor courts etc will have NO jurisdiction in these plots as these plots will be exempt from labor laws , PF laws etc etc.
.
Again, please note that exact details of how how "free" these plots will be are yet to come. Above are based on previous deals Govt of Gujarat and Govt of India made with Industrial Parks in Gujarat and rest of India.
.
Now Indian factory owners will have to bear burden of labor laws, PF laws income tax, stamp duty, municipal taxes, excise etc. And he will have to work under Municipal laws wrt construction etc. And in most cases, he will get poor usual poor municipal services of roads, public buses etc. So there is no way he can be as productive as the factory owners in these foreign industrial parks. So he will he die out or will have to make partnerships with foreign companies.
.
Solutions I propose are - (a) uniform laws for foreigners as well as Indian factory owners (b) print law-drafts to reduce inefficiencies of Indian factory owners , law-drafts such as Jury System, TCP, RTR, DDMRCAM, Wealth tax , Inheritance tax, repeal VAT, repeal GST, repeal Service Tax etc etc. I have discussed all this in chap-20 of rahulmehta.com / 301.htm .
.
=====
.
2. Ukraine rents away 5% of its land to Chinese !!
.
Please google on "Ukraine China land". Please also see links in first comment of this status. It says "Under the 50-year plan, China would eventually control three million hectares ... which represents nine per cent of Ukraine's arable land. Initially 100,000 hectares would be leased. The farmland in the eastern Dnipropetrovsk region would be cultivated principally for growing crops and raising pigs. The produce will be sold at preferential prices to Chinese state-owned conglomerates, said the Xinjiang Production and Construction Corp (XPCC), a quasi-military organisation also known as Bingtuan"
.
GoI has already started giving away plots to Chinese , Japanese etc in the name of Industrial Parks. These plots are only of size of "few"thousand acres. Lets hope that GoI doesnt do a Ukraine in India !!
.
===
.
3. In my next status, I will explain how Sonia = NaMo = AK and workers of Congress-RSS-BJP-AAP-BST etc have convinced average Indian and youth that only foreigners can build India? And how they have convinced Indian youth that we Swadeshies are all idiots.
.
-Rahul Chimanbhai Mehta , Right to recall Group

(b) If the decide to keep profits in India, then that profit will be tax exempt
.
(c) ONLY Chinese can setup factories there.
.
(d) If an Indian wishes to setup factory, restaurant or anything,, then that Indian will need permission of Chinese body which "owns" that plot.
.
(e) And inside that plot, all municipal services such as water, roads, internal buses, garbage, sewere etc will be run by Chinese companies.
.
(f) The Municipalities will have no jurisdiction and can collect property taxes there !!
.
(g). And they wont need to pay stamp duty to State Govt for internal sales !!
.
(h) While jurisdiction of most Indian courts will apply, many courts such as labor courts etc will have NO jurisdiction in these plots as these plots will be exempt from labor laws , PF laws etc etc.
.
Again, please note that exact details of how how "free" these plots will be are yet to come. Above are based on previous deals Govt of Gujarat and Govt of India made with Industrial Parks in Gujarat and rest of India.
.
Now Indian factory owners will have to bear burden of labor laws, PF laws income tax, stamp duty, municipal taxes, excise etc. And he will have to work under Municipal laws wrt construction etc. And in most cases, he will get poor usual poor municipal services of roads, public buses etc. So there is no way he can be as productive as the factory owners in these foreign industrial parks. So he will he die out or will have to make partnerships with foreign companies.
.
Solutions I propose are - (a) uniform laws for foreigners as well as Indian factory owners (b) print law-drafts to reduce inefficiencies of Indian factory owners , law-drafts such as Jury System, TCP, RTR, DDMRCAM, Wealth tax , Inheritance tax, repeal VAT, repeal GST, repeal Service Tax etc etc. I have discussed all this in chap-20 of rahulmehta.com / 301.htm .
.
=====
.
2. Ukraine rents away 5% of its land to Chinese !!
.
Please google on "Ukraine China land". Please also see links in first comment of this status. It says "Under the 50-year plan, China would eventually control three million hectares ... which represents nine per cent of Ukraine's arable land. Initially 100,000 hectares would be leased. The farmland in the eastern Dnipropetrovsk region would be cultivated principally for growing crops and raising pigs. The produce will be sold at preferential prices to Chinese state-owned conglomerates, said the Xinjiang Production and Construction Corp (XPCC), a quasi-military organisation also known as Bingtuan"
.
GoI has already started giving away plots to Chinese , Japanese etc in the name of Industrial Parks. These plots are only of size of "few"thousand acres. Lets hope that GoI doesnt do a Ukraine in India !!
.
===
.

.
-Rahul Chimanbhai Mehta , Right to recall Group

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Jun 26 2014 13:08:07 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Understaing Railways ---- why price rise is must and why FDI in rwailys is just a sell out, and why even 1% FDI is not needed at all. Solution I propose is to raise ALL tickets prices by 4 times, and directly send Rs 300 per citizen per year to every citizen !! That way both railways and poor will be better off then they are. And rs 300 is NOT freebee --- it is less that rent of land which belongs to the citizens anyway
.
A must see statistical summary link is at indianrailways. gov.in / railwayboard/uploads/directorate/stat_econ/pdf/Summary%20Sheet_Eng.pdf
.
ppkm = per passanger-km.

Railway charges 27 paise per passenger-km !!! Yes, it is NOT Rs 27 per passenger-km. But it is only 27 paise per passenger-km. And that includes the luggage passengers carry !!! And in northern states, some half the passengers dont tale tickets. So actual price is much less !!
.
And in 1951, it was 1.25 paise. ppkm = per passenger km. So 500 km Ahmedabad-Mumbai journey will be about Rs 6 to Rs 7 only. But that was Rs 6 of those days. Gold was Rs 10 per gram back then and now it is Rs 3000 per gram. So gold prices has gone up 300 times. So by that account, pries of railways should have been Rs 4.75 ppkm. = some 15 times the existing prices. And rupee volume in 1951 was Rs 60 per Indian citizen. Today, it is Rs 60000 per Indian citizen, including population increase. So by that measure, price should be Rs 10 ppkm = some 35 times the existing price . But it is ONLY 27 paise ppkm.
.
IMO, the prices must be Re 1 per km at least. . What about poor? Well, with 4 times the ticker prices, the passanger ticket revenue will increase from Rs 30000 crore to Rs 120,000 crore. So about 30% of the revenue i.e. about Rs 300 per per citizen per year can be DIRECTLY sent to each citizen .Thats isnt charity. It is land rent for the land commons own and have given to railways,. And even after that, poor and railways will be better off.
.
Pls study the PDF and you will get a much better picture.
.
So we really dont need any FDI nonsense in railway. A raising prices to a rational level, direct cash pay outs of say 25% revenue and fixing corruption is MORE than sufficient,

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Jun 26 2014 19:09:33 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Back of the envelope calculations show that hige amount of diesel seems to be getting stolen from railways !! To get exact numbers, one will need to get full trainwise consumption.
.
solution is to use dyes. There are harmless dyes and maskers which can be mixed in disel and they make the smoke colored. Say red color is used. Then if someone steals that dye and used that disel in truck, then that truck will emit red smoke which can be easily seen and detected even from a distance. And then marker can prove the exact source. That will cust down diesel theft from railways and also city\state transport.
.
Another "theft" from railways is a large number of ticket less passengers. That can be stopped by dismissing all TCs = Ticket Checkers !! And use 100% private commission based TCs and Jury System, There are many technical solutions to reduce free-loading.
.
All in all, ways to fix railways are there, FDI is farce. No FDi is needed to improve railways.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Fri Jun 27 2014 06:41:57 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Shailendra Rathore Rtr:

अधिकाँश मोदी भगत रक्षा में FDI का समर्थन इस आधार पर कर रहे है कि -

-इस से भारत में हथियार बनाने की तकनीक आएगी
- रोजगार बढेगा ।
- अर्थ शास्त्री इसे अच्छा कदम बता रहे है
- सेना को मज़बूत बनाने का एक मात्र यही उपाय है आदि आदि

मोदी भगतो से कुछ सवाल :

1. मोदी ने पहले एक महीने में कोन से ऐसे क़ानून पास किये जिनसे भारत हथियारों में आत्मनिर्भर बने ?

2. बीजेपी के अपकमिंग क़ानून बताये जिनसे हथियारों कअ उत्पादन करने की तकनीक भारत में उन्नत होगी ?

3. टेक्नोलोजी ट्रान्सफर एक मिथक है । खासकर हथियारों में । क्योकि कोई भी कंपनी पूरा वेपन खुद नही बना सकती । शेव्रोन सोफ्टवेयर बनाती है तो दूपों सर्किट जबकि इंजन रोल्स रोयस । एक हथियार की तकनीक 1000 इंजिनियर और 10 देशो में बिखरी पड़ी है ।
कम्पनी सिर्फ असेम्ब्ले करती है । तो तकनीक ट्रांसफर कैसे होगी ?

4. चीन की सेना 1950 में हमसे कमज़ोर थी लेकिन आज मज़बूत है । चीन ने बिना FDI केसे आत्म्निर्भरता प्राप्त की ?

5. इन कम्पनियों के एकाधिकार को आप केसे रोकेंगे ताकि ये भारतीय हथियार कम्पनियों को निगल नही पाए ?

6. ये जो मुनाफा कमाने वाली है उनका डॉलर में पुनार्भरन कैसे करेंगे ?

7. सभी अर्थशास्त्री पेड है इसलिए हमें इस से सरोकार नही कि किसने कहा , हमे आप ये बताइये कि 'क्या कहा' ?

8. युद्ध के समय यदि ये कम्पनिय यदि हअथियारो के दाम 4 गुना कर देती ह तो आप के पास क्या विकल्प है ?

9. आप इन्हें देश में विज्ञान एवं गणित की शिक्षा का बेस तोड़ने से कैसे रोकेंगे ?

10. MOU में एक मेंड़ेट्री क्लाज़ होता ह ' tech wd be transferable as pr US govt rule' यदि NATO ने बाद में प्रस्ताव पास कर दिया कि कॉम्पेक्स वेपन्स की टेक्नोलोजी ट्रांसफर नही होगी तो आप के पास क्या विकल्प है ?

11. and ths one is belongs to complt common sence.. अमेरिका महाशक्ति इसलिए है क्योकि उसके पास सबसे उन्नत हथियार है । आप के इस इस विश्वाश का आधार क्या है कि अमेरिका आपको उन्नत राडार, हाई रेंज क्रूज़ मिसाईल, लड़ाकू विमान, लेसर गाइडेड मिसाईल आदि की टेक्नोलोजी देगा । जो कि उसकी शक्ति का आधार है ? यदि अब तक किसी देश को दी हो तो मिसाल बताइये ?

प्रजा अधीन राजा @ shailsa...

===
.
1. Please see link in the first comment. It says that Supriya Sule, daughter of Sharad Pawar, earned Rs 113 crore as farm income, from land of only 10 acres. Basically, many neta-babu-IPS-judges-elitemen etc show their black income as farm income, as it converts into white at the tax rate of 0% !! I have been campaigning for solution to this mess in chap-25 of rahulmehta . com / 301.htm in 1998 ., and had also given the solution in newspaper advt in Indian Express delhi edition on first page in jan-2009. The solution is to tax agricultuiral income above Rs 100,000 per family member. This will reduce tax burden on the rest. Now SoNaKe (*) and CoBhApSwaRa have been opposing this tax on agricultural income. So if you want agricultural income to be taxed at par, then please order your SMS to remove tax exemption to agricultural income above Rs 100,000 per person. And please dont waste time with workers of CoBhApSwaRa as they all support unlimited tax exemption to farm income.
.
2. Motabhai had bribed everyone in Congress = BJP. So all CoBha leaders had agreed to support massive price rise increase in natural gas. Rockefellar asked his agent AK to oppose it and Rockefellar also paid mediamen to project AK = AAP. And so gas price increase got postponed. .
Now who should get credit of delay in price rise? IMO, the real credit goes to Rockefellar etc, The role of AK was no different from an actor.
.
Consider a superhit science fiction movie like Stars Wars or Edge of Tomorrow. Such movies are appealing because of script, hard work by computer engineers who create computer generated graphical images, the mathematicians who write algorithms. And financiers who take the financial ris, and director/producers who collect the pieces. The actors, be Tom Cruise or anyone, are only frontsmen who read the script and create good facial expressions. Any person who spends times in studying the whole movies will see that actors deserve least credit.
.
Same way, the entire media funding against gas price rise was done by Rockefellar. AK only read the script written by Ford Foundation (FF is also Rockefellar front) and created good facial expression. His background created an image of seriousness. But all that was 0.5%. without paidmedia coverage, his voice would have reached not more than 1000 Indian voters. And AK is wise. He saw that Rockefellar will fund his tirade against Reliance gas price rise and so he took that issue. But AK refused to ask AAP MPs to submit law-drafts against FDI in railway, which is 10 times more damaging than gas price rise. Because he saw that MNC-owners will NOT fund media coverage for this. And AK also doesnt speak against EVMs and doesnt want to put 65 dummy independents to defeat EVMs, as MNC-owners do NOT want that.
.
So credit of averting gad price rise goes ONLY to Rockefellar.
.
Now we cant depend on Rockefellar for such work, be good or bad. Solution is law-draft that would increase competition amongst Indian companies in gas exploration field. I will publish my proposed law-drafts soon. Lets see what lawdrafts SoNaKe and CoBhApSwaRa propose.
.

Such movies are appealing because of script, hard work by computer engineers who create computer generated graphical images, the mathematicians who write algorithms. And financiers who take the financial ris, and director/producers who collect the pieces. The actors, be Tom Cruise or anyone, are only frontsmen who read the script and create good facial expressions. Any person who spends times in studying the whole movies will see that actors deserve least credit.
.
Same way, the entire media funding against gas price rise was done by Rockefellar. AK only read the script written by Ford Foundation (FF is also Rockefellar front) and created good facial expression. His background created an image of seriousness. But all that was 0.5%. without paidmedia coverage, his voice would have reached not more than 1000 Indian voters. And AK is wise. He saw that Rockefellar will fund his tirade against Reliance gas price rise and so he took that issue. But AK refused to ask AAP MPs to submit law-drafts against FDI in railway, which is 10 times more damaging than gas price rise. Because he saw that MNC-owners will NOT fund media coverage for this. And AK also doesnt speak against EVMs and doesnt want to put 65 dummy independents to defeat EVMs, as MNC-owners do NOT want that.
.
So credit of averting gad price rise goes ONLY to Rockefellar.
.
Now we cant depend on Rockefellar for such work, be good or bad. Solution is law-draft that would increase competition amongst Indian companies in gas exploration field. I will publish my proposed law-drafts soon. Lets see what lawdrafts SoNaKe and CoBhApSwaRa propose.
.
-Rahul Chimanbhai Mehta , Right to recall Group
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sat Jun 28 2014 12:56:05 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

आपको मालूम है हनुमान जी इतने सकसेज़ क्यों हुए ? 📍📍

कैसे वो पहाड़ उखाड़ कर ला पाए ?
( हांलाकि सुषेण और लछमण जी को कंधो पर उठा के ले जाते तो वजन भी कम रहता और पहाड़ भी डिस्टर्ब नही होता🎭 , लछमण को बूटी भी ज़ल्दी मिलती और भरत का तीर भी नही खाना पड़ता । पर श्री राम चन्द्र जी ने कहा था कि बूटी तो यहीं लगेगी , इसलिए .. 🌱🌱और प्रभु के लिए क्या कहें.. वो तो लीला कर रहे थे , और लीला तो अपरंपार ही होती है , उसका कोई ओर छोर नही होता ) 😎

कैसे उन्होंने लंका में आग लगा दी ( पर पूंछ में नही लगने दी )

तो वो ऐसा कैसे कर पाते थे ।
इस पर काफी सिर मग्जन करने से एक ही कारण समझ आता है , जिस कारण बालाजी की शक्तिया अपरिमित रूप से बढ़ गयी थी ।

और वो कारण था भक्ति ।😴😴😴 एक्चुली हनुमान जी ' भगत' आदमी थे । बस इसी से पूरा सुन्दर काण्ड कर डाला था उन्होंने ।
भक्ति में बहुत शक्ति होती है ।

ऐसे ही एक और केरेक्टर थे । विभीषण ।
विभीषण भी भगत आदमी थे । हनुमान जब लंका फूंकने गए थे, तभी उन्होंने ताड़ लिया था कि लंका से ये FDI लगाईं जा सकती है । 😆😆😆😆
विभीषण भी लंका के फुंकने के बाद निवेश के लिए बढ़िया टेरेटरी ढून्ढ रहे थे । तो सेटिंग बैठ गयी ।

भगतो में सेटिंग फटाफट हो जाती है । 👬👬

बाली भी एक ताक़तवर राजा था , पर बाली ने सोचा भी नही था कि सुग्रीव श्री राम से मित्रता की आड़ में इत्ती भयंकर FDI लगा रहा है , 👺जो कि उसे नज़र भी तीर खाने के बाद आने वाली थी । 😹😹😹😹😹

वेसे रावण के विनाश और लंका ध्वंस का मूल कारण भी FDI ही था । न वो परस्त्री धन पर नज़र डालता न उसका विनाश होता ।

महाभारत में भी कौरवों ने शकुनी नाम की लॉन्ग टर्म FDI लगा रखी थी । और इस एक FDI ने सारे कौरवों को हस्तिनापुर समेत सल्टा दिया । 🙈🙈🙈🙉🙉🙉

लब्बो लुआब यह कि जहाँ भी FDI लगती है अल्टीमेटली सूपड़े साफ़ हो जाते है । 🌍🌎🌏

पर भगत लोग इतने चक्कर में नही पड़ते, भगत लोगो का इंटरेस्ट FDI में नही होता , उनकी रुचि ये जानने में होती है कि FDI लगाईं किसने है ?

अगर 'प्रभु' ने लगाईं है तो FDI क्या FFDI से भी कोई दिक्कत नही है । प्रभु इच्छा प्रबल है । भगत जो ठहरे ।

(someone is asking 'wt the hell is FFDI'.. ? even we cd nt able to undrstand FDI... FD is enough ! 'may be fixed deposit' )

पर निर्मल बाबा का कहना है कि 'भगती' सही जगह इस्तेमाल करो, देश पर कृपा आनी चालू हो जायेगी ।

हर हर FDI घर घर FDI
